Here are the decklists for the Stipulation Cube portion of the 2015 Community Cup! Descriptions of the stipulations [can be found here](https://web.archive.org/web/20150919000000/http://magic.wizards.com/node/709271). 

#### Community: Gabe "Doc" Reale - Odds or Evens
